value","ref":"83:22","note":"Generic na'im is redundant and does not focus privileged denial."},{"label":"medium","ref":"6:5","note":"Rejection of the truth is followed by future disclosure of what was mocked."},{"label":"no value","ref":"53:34","note":"Qaleelan describes limited giving, not a period granted to deniers."},{"label":"no value","ref":"13:10","note":"Its night-and-day contrast does not contribute to the focus."},{"label":"no value","ref":"55:32","note":"Another repeated denial question contributes no new focused evidence."},{"label":"weak","ref":"5:44","note":"The small price for signs offers a broad value contrast, not bounded reprieve."},{"label":"weak","ref":"26:141","note":"A historical denier label is redundant after more developed denial parallels."},{"label":"no value","ref":"33:20","note":"The small amount of fighting is unrelated to the focus's qaleelan."},{"label":"medium","ref":"43:25","note":"Explicitly traces deniers to a retaliatory outcome, reinforcing the judgment trajectory."},{"label":"no value","ref":"89:2","note":"The numbered nights do not focus the command or its target group."},{"label":"no value","ref":"53:11","note":"Negated falsehood of the heart differs from the deniers addressed in 73:11."},{"label":"strong","ref":"52:45","note":"Directly tells the messenger to leave them until their appointed day."},{"label":"strong","ref":"70:42","note":"Directly tells the messenger to leave them in diversion until they meet their day."},{"label":"strong","ref":"43:83","note":"Directly couples leaving them with an appointed future meeting."},{"label":"strong","ref":"2:126","note":"Explicitly grants brief enjoyment before compulsion toward punishment."},{"label":"strong","ref":"3:178","note":"Treats extended allowance to disbelievers as serving a worsening outcome."},{"label":"strong","ref":"7:182","note":"States an unperceived, gradual drawing-on of those who deny signs."},{"label":"strong","ref":"7:183","note":"Adds deliberate respite and firm planning to the prior gradual process."},{"label":"strong","ref":"13:32","note":"Pairs allowance to disbelievers with their later seizure."},{"label":"strong","ref":"22:44","note":"Pairs allowance to disbelievers with a later repudiating seizure."},{"label":"strong","ref":"68:45","note":"Near 68:44, deliberate respite is explicitly joined to firm planning."},{"label":"strong","ref":"17:16","note":"Links privileged inhabitants' corruption to a community's decreed destruction."},{"label":"strong","ref":"21:13","note":"Addresses those indulged in ease as they flee an arriving destructive outcome."},{"label":"strong","ref":"23:33","note":"Combines disbelief, denial of the final meeting, and worldly indulgence in one group."},{"label":"strong","ref":"23:64","note":"Shows the seizure of the indulged leaders as the end of their false confidence."},{"label":"strong","ref":"34:34","note":"Makes affluent town leaders the recurring speakers who reject a messenger's mission."},{"label":"strong","ref":"43:23","note":"Again makes affluent town leaders preserve and oppose a messenger's call."},{"label":"strong","ref":"56:45","note":"Identifies prior indulgence as the setting for grave persistence."},{"label":"strong","ref":"74:12","note":"Begins the immediately following account of the singled-out man's extended wealth."},{"label":"strong","ref":"74:13","note":"Adds sons in public attendance to the account of privileged endowment."},{"label":"strong","ref":"74:14","note":"Completes that account with abundant facilitation before the threat unfolds."},{"label":"medium","ref":"74:15","note":"Shows the privileged figure's continuing demand for further increase."},{"label":"medium","ref":"11:116","note":"Connects wrongdoing with following what had indulged them."},{"label":"medium","ref":"34:35","note":"Has the affluent claim that wealth and children protect them from punishment."},{"label":"medium","ref":"23:55","note":"Questions whether repeated wealth and sons should be read as unqualified good."},{"label":"medium","ref":"23:56","note":"Continues that question by denying the targets' grasp of its meaning."},{"label":"medium","ref":"35:45","note":"Sets deferred reckoning against an appointed term rather than denial of judgment."},{"label":"medium","ref":"16:61","note":"Describes postponement until an appointed term despite wrongdoing."},{"label":"medium","ref":"2:15","note":"Portrays the deniers' extension as continuing bewilderment, not vindication."},{"label":"medium","ref":"6:70","note":"Directly commands leaving those who trivialize religion, with a warning of consequence."},{"label":"medium","ref":"6:91","note":"Directly commands leaving them in their diversion after the truth is presented."},{"label":"medium","ref":"6:137","note":"Directly commands leaving them with their fabrication under divine non-intervention."},{"label":"weak","ref":"7:180","note":"Directly commands leaving a different class of opponents, without the reprieve frame."},{"label":"medium","ref":"35:4","note":"Addresses the messenger's being denied by locating it in the earlier messenger pattern."},{"label":"medium","ref":"3:184","note":"Treats denial of the messenger as repetition of denial faced by earlier messengers."},{"label":"medium","ref":"16:113","note":"Links a messenger's arrival, denial, and seizure in a compact precedent."},{"label":"medium","ref":"29:18","note":"Places current denial within prior communities' denial while limiting the messenger's role."},{"label":"medium","ref":"40:5","note":"Combines communal denial of messengers with an attempted seizure and decisive outcome."},{"label":"medium","ref":"7:95","note":"Shows ease and abundance mistaken for security immediately before sudden seizure."},{"label":"medium","ref":"44:25","note":"Displays abandoned gardens and sources as the aftermath of a destroyed affluent setting."},{"label":"medium","ref":"26:146","note":"Questions a rejecting people about continued security amid gardens and springs."},{"label":"medium","ref":"18:32","note":"Introduces concentrated garden wealth as a setting for the subsequent warning narrative."},{"label":"medium","ref":"18:35","note":"Shows a garden owner wronging himself amid prosperity and denying its loss."},{"label":"medium","ref":"102:1","note":"Competition in accumulation supplies a secondary frame for heedless abundance."},{"label":"medium","ref":"104:2","note":"Treats amassed, counted wealth as a false basis for imagined permanence."},{"label":"weak","ref":"89:15","note":"Records the human tendency to equate generous provision with honor."},{"label":"weak","ref":"89:16","note":"Records the corresponding tendency to treat measured provision as humiliation."}],"channel_subchannels_anchored_here":[{"parent_channel":"P2 - Deferred Appointment and Irreversible Arrival","parent_semantic_invariant":"Delay belongs to a fixed schedule whose promised event ultimately arrives, occurs, and acquires the force of an accomplished act.","parent_surprising_reach":"Temporal reckoning extends into moon stations, lunar halos, star markers, weekday naming, and the contrast between first, later, and terminal positions.","key":"A","name":"A short reprieve before an appointed time","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"A period of calm delay is granted, but it remains bounded by a specified appointment.","active_motifs":"deliberate delay and absence of haste (`quranic:root_001452:B001/m01`); a small interval (`quranic:root_001251:B001/m01`); postponement to a later time (`quranic:root_000019:B002/m01`); an appointment fixed by time or place (`quranic:root_001662:B003/m01`); a duration of time (`quranic:root_001700:B002/m01`); an event present in time (`quranic:root_001332:B001/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"73:11 `مَهِّلْهُمْ/قَلِيلًا`; 73:14,17 `يَوْمَ/يَوْمًا`; 73:18 `كَانَ/وَعْدُهُ`; 73:20 `ءَاخَرُونَ/يَكُونُ`","synthesis":"Delay is not cancellation but a measured opening before an appointment. “Later” and temporal occurrence keep the reprieve inside a schedule whose endpoint remains intact.","ayah_refs":["73:11","73:14","73:18","73:20"]},{"parent_channel":"P2 - Deferred Appointment and Irreversible Arrival","parent_semantic_invariant":"Delay belongs to a fixed schedule whose promised event ultimately arrives, occurs, and acquires the force of an accomplished act.","parent_surprising_reach":"Temporal reckoning extends into moon stations, lunar halos, star markers, weekday naming, and the contrast between first, later, and terminal positions.","key":"B","name":"Promise becoming an accomplished event","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"A verbal commitment advances from expectation through arrival to enacted fact.","active_motifs":"a promise opening expectation (`quranic:root_001662:B001/m01`); threatening promise (`quranic:root_001662:B002/m01`); coming and arrival (`quranic:root_000009:B001/m01`); calamity arriving upon a person (`quranic:root_000009:B011/m01`); return to an outcome or final meaning (`quranic:root_000067:B002/m01`); occurrence in time (`quranic:root_001332:B001/m01`); an enacted deed (`quranic:root_001167:B001/m01`); a calamitous day or event (`quranic:root_001700:B003/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"73:11 `أُو۟لِى`; 73:14,17 `يَوْمَ/يَوْمًا`; 73:18 `كَانَ/وَعْدُهُ/مَفْعُولًا`; 73:20 `ءَاتُ/يَكُونُ`","synthesis":"The sequence closes the distance between saying and reality. Arrival supplies the transition, occurrence supplies presence, action realizes it, and outcome names what the event finally becomes or means.","ayah_refs":["73:11","73:14","73:18","73:20"]},{"parent_channel":"P2 - Deferred Appointment and Irreversible Arrival","parent_semantic_invariant":"Delay belongs to a fixed schedule whose promised event ultimately arrives, occurs, and acquires the force of an accomplished act.","parent_surprising_reach":"Temporal reckoning extends into moon stations, lunar halos, star markers, weekday naming, and the contrast between first, later, and terminal positions.","key":"D","name":"First, later, forward, and rear positions","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A scheduled sequence distinguishes its beginning and predecessors from later, forward, and rearward positions.","active_motifs":"beginning or first position (`quranic:root_000067:B001/m01`); precedence in age, merit, or movement (`quranic:root_001452:B002/m01`); what comes after the first (`quranic:root_000019:B001/m01`); a rearward position (`quranic:root_000019:B003/m01`); forward advance or precedence (`quranic:root_001207:B004/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"73:11 `مَهِّلْهُمْ/أُو۟لِى`; 73:20 `ءَاخَرُونَ/تُقَدِّمُوا`","synthesis":"Temporal order is made spatially legible. First and later establish sequence, precedence and advance establish relative movement, and the rear establishes the opposite position; deferred arrival therefore remains an ordered displacement, not an indefinite pause.","ayah_refs":["73:11","73:20"]},{"parent_channel":"P2 - Punitive Closure of Movement and Intake","parent_semantic_invariant":"Punishment closes ordinary passages: limbs cannot move, the throat cannot pass food, and heat cannot be escaped.","parent_surprising_reach":"Fetters, a wrestling hold, a neck grip, an unwholesome meal, molten residue, and war-fire form one system of bodily closure rather than separate punishment images.","key":"C","name":"Enclosure by fire and molten heat","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"Fire, molten matter, and extreme heat surround the punished body.","active_motifs":"blazing infernal fire (`quranic:root_000225:B001/m01`); exposure to fire and its heat (`quranic:root_000879:B001/m01`); molten metal, oily dregs, or pus-like residue (`quranic:root_001452:B003/m01`); punishment by severe pain (`quranic:root_000994:B005/m01`); harmful heaviness (`quranic:root_001619:B002/m01`); clamor of fire and war (`quranic:root_001434:B001/m01`); marching in extreme heat (`quranic:root_001434:B002/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"73:11 `مَهِّلْهُمْ`; 73:12 `جَحِيمًا`; 73:13 `عَذَابًا`; 73:16 `وَبِيلًا`; 73:20 `صَّلَوٰةَ`; surface anchors unavailable for `quranic:root_001434:B001` and `quranic:root_001434:B002` because `م ع ع` is missing from root coverage","synthesis":"The scene combines radiant heat, direct burning, molten residue, and suffocating clamor. Even movement through the environment becomes a march inside heat, completing the closure of escape.","ayah_refs":["73:11","73:12","73:13","73:16","73:20"]},{"parent_channel":"P2 - Message Tested by Testimony and Enactment","parent_semantic_invariant":"A communicated claim is tested by witness, enacted fact, and the quality of its recipients’ response.","parent_surprising_reach":"Falsehood appears not only as lying but as a failed charge, deceptive cloth, rumor-induced panic, and fabricated composition.","key":"B","name":"Denial, rumor, and deceptive representation","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"Recipients reject the message while false representations circulate and generate disturbance.","active_motifs":"falsehood opposed to truth (`quranic:root_001290:B001/m01`); attributing falsehood to a speaker (`quranic:root_001290:B002/m01`); a deceptive dyed or patterned cloth (`quranic:root_001290:B009/m01`); denial and concealment of truth (`quranic:root_001307:B003/m01`); alarming or false rumor (`quranic:root_000545:B003/m01`); fabricated discourse made without a prior model (`quranic:root_001167:B004/m01`); disobedience to authority (`quranic:root_001022:B001/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"73:11 `ٱلْمُكَذِّبِينَ`; 73:14 `تَرْجُفُ`; 73:16 `عَصَىٰ`; 73:17 `كَفَرْتُمْ`; 73:18 `مَفْعُولًا`","synthesis":"The scene distinguishes several failures of representation: direct lying, accusing another of lying, visual deception, fabricated composition, and rumor. Disobedience is the behavioral outcome when that representational refusal is directed at an envoy.","ayah_refs":["73:11","73:14","73:16","73:17","73:18"]},{"parent_channel":"P2 - Message Tested by Testimony and Enactment","parent_semantic_invariant":"A communicated claim is tested by witness, enacted fact, and the quality of its recipients’ response.","parent_surprising_reach":"Falsehood appears not only as lying but as a failed charge, deceptive cloth, rumor-induced panic, and fabricated composition.","key":"D","name":"Answer, assent, and unstable alignment","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A recipient answers affirmatively, then either embodies submission or merely aligns with whichever side prevails.","active_motifs":"saying yes in confirmation or promise (`quranic:root_001525:B004/m01`); answering a call (`quranic:root_001573:B003/m01`); bodily gesture of submission (`quranic:root_001307:B014/m01`); submissive yielding (`quranic:root_001332:B004/m01`); siding with the victor without a settled position (`quranic:root_001434:B005/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"73:11 `ٱلنَّعْمَةِ`; 73:14,18,20 `كَانَتِ/كَانَ/يَكُونُ`; 73:17 `كَفَرْتُمْ`; surface anchor unavailable for `quranic:root_001573:B003` because `ه ا ء` is missing from root coverage, and for `quranic:root_001434:B005` because `م ع ع` is missing from root coverage","synthesis":"Verbal yes, responsive answer, bodily deference, and inward yielding can align, but the opportunistic partisan exposes their possible separation. Recipient response is therefore tested not by assent alone but by whether word, posture, and durable position correspond.","ayah_refs":["73:11","73:14","73:17"]},{"parent_channel":"P2 - Compressed Life Cycle","parent_semantic_invariant":"Birth, growth, maturation, and aging can be accelerated until distant life stages collapse into one event.","parent_surprising_reach":"The child-to-grey transformation is supported by a detailed reproductive ledger of womb, afterbirth, childbirth blood, near delivery, tooth eruption, and derived new life.","key":"D","name":"Lineage, household, and age cohort","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A child enters a network of parents, kin, household dependents, caretakers, and age peers.","active_motifs":"parents (`quranic:root_001683:B002/m01`); kinship and family bond (`quranic:root_000552:B002/m01`); household or attached followers (`quranic:root_000067:B003/m01`); a caretaker and stepchild relation (`quranic:root_000532:B005/m01`); an age peer (`quranic:root_001683:B006/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"73:11 `أُو۟لِى`; 73:17 `ٱلْوِلْدَٰنَ`; 73:19-20 `رَبِّهِ/رَبَّكَ`; 73:20 `رَّحِيمٌ`","synthesis":"Birth does not produce an isolated body. It inserts the child into vertical descent, lateral kinship, domestic care, and a same-age cohort; the sudden greying therefore also disrupts the relational positions by which age is ordinarily recognized.","ayah_refs":["73:11","73:17","73:19","73:20"]},{"parent_channel":"P2 - Measured Contribution and Returned Value","parent_semantic_invariant":"A contribution is first apportioned, then transferred, and finally recovered as value, benefit, or reward.","parent_surprising_reach":"Night fractions and devotional giving share the same operational grammar of amount, ledger, advance, loan, equivalent return, and multiplied value.","key":"A","name":"Portioning and counting the night","reading_type":"surface-primary","scene_or_process":"A duration is divided into exact fractions, estimated, counted, and adjusted to a manageable amount.","active_motifs":"the number three (`quranic:root_000203:B001/m01`); a third portion (`quranic:root_000203:B002/m01`); one half (`quranic:root_001511:B001/m01`); reaching a midpoint (`quranic:root_001511:B002/m01`); specified quantity and limit (`quranic:root_001205:B001/m01`); planning by calculation (`quranic:root_001205:B005/m01`); exhaustive counting (`quranic:root_000332:B002/m01`); equal weight or moderate quantity (`quranic:root_001273:B015/m01`); a small amount (`quranic:root_001251:B001/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"73:11 `قَلِيلًا`; 73:20 `ثُلُثَىِ/نِصْفَهُ/ثُلُثَهُ/يُقَدِّرُ/تُحْصُوهُ/تَقُومُ`","synthesis":"Number names identify the portions, midpoint locates them, measure sets their limit, and calculation attempts an exact ledger. Fewness and moderate weight allow the same system to reduce the burden when exhaustive counting fails.","ayah_refs":["73:11","73:20"]},{"parent_channel":"P2 - Provision Ecology","parent_semantic_invariant":"Provision moves through land, rain, crop, livestock, processing, and social distribution.","parent_surprising_reach":"The bundle assembles both a crop cycle and a dairy herd cycle beneath the surah’s contrast between luxury, punitive food, livelihood, and charitable giving.","key":"B","name":"Herd, milk, and animal nourishment","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Livestock reproduce, fill the udder, release milk, gain fat, and ruminate.","active_motifs":"livestock wealth (`quranic:root_001525:B005/m01`); a she-camel filling three vessels or milking from three teats (`quranic:root_000203:B005/m01`); abundant milk and offspring (`quranic:root_001694:B006/m01`); continuous milk flow (`quranic:root_000563:B006/m01`); fat in animal flesh and marrow (`quranic:root_000934:B007/m01`); a camel chewing cud (`quranic:root_001217:B008/m01`); milk failing after appearing durable (`quranic:root_001290:B006/m01`); mating drive in sheep (`quranic:root_001619:B006/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"73:11 `ٱلنَّعْمَةِ`; 73:13 `طَعَامًا`; 73:15-16 `أَرْسَلْنَا/ٱلرَّسُولَ/وَبِيلًا`; 73:20 `ثُلُثَىِ/تَيَسَّرَ/قَرْضًا`","synthesis":"Reproduction initiates a dairy chain whose products are milk, fat, and sustained animal nourishment. The failed-milk motif marks the system’s vulnerability, contrasting real provision with an appearance that does not last.","ayah_refs":["73:11","73:13","73:15","73:20"]},{"parent_channel":"P2 - Provision Ecology","parent_semantic_invariant":"Provision moves through land, rain, crop, livestock, processing, and social distribution.","parent_surprising_reach":"The bundle assembles both a crop cycle and a dairy herd cycle beneath the surah’s contrast between luxury, punitive food, livelihood, and charitable giving.","key":"C","name":"Food, livelihood, and generous distribution","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"Provision is acquired as livelihood, served to others, and redistributed as surplus or gift.","active_motifs":"feeding another and responding to need (`quranic:root_000934:B002/m01`); food as livelihood and good condition (`quranic:root_000934:B004/m01`); surplus after need (`quranic:root_001163:B001/m01`); generous gift (`quranic:root_001163:B003/m01`); generosity and donation (`quranic:root_000452:B005/m01`); wealth and practical means (`quranic:root_001626:B003/m01`); giving (`quranic:root_000009:B002/m01`); blessing and good condition (`quranic:root_001525:B001/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"73:11 `ٱلنَّعْمَةِ`; 73:13 `طَعَامًا`; 73:20 `ءَاتُوا/يَبْتَغُونَ/فَضْلِ/خَيْرٍ/تَجِدُوهُ`","synthesis":"Acquisition is only the first stage. Livelihood produces surplus, surplus enables feeding and gift, and giving circulates provision back through the social field.","ayah_refs":["73:11","73:13","73:20"]},{"parent_channel":"P2 - Hearth and Meal Preparation","parent_semantic_invariant":"A meal is made by assembling a hearth, applying heat, cutting material, and controlling concentration.","parent_surprising_reach":"A pot, third hearthstone, lifting cloth, cut meat, fire, hastily baked dough, reduced drink, and measured sips form an ordinary kitchen whose operations are inverted by the punitive meal.","key":"A","name":"Building and working the hearth","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A pot is set on three supports, heated, handled with a cloth, and supplied with cut food.","active_motifs":"cooking pot and its contents (`quranic:root_001205:B007/m01`); the third hearthstone completing a support (`quranic:root_000203:B007/m01`); cloth used to lower or lift a hot pot (`quranic:root_000248:B007/m01`); cutting meat (`quranic:root_001638:B002/m01`); exposure to cooking fire (`quranic:root_000879:B001/m01`); blazing heat (`quranic:root_000225:B001/m01`); dough baked before proper maturation (`quranic:root_001165:B006/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"73:11 `مَهِّلْهُمْ`; 73:12 `جَحِيمًا`; 73:14 `ٱلْجِبَالُ`; 73:17 `يَجْعَلُ`; 73:18 `مُنفَطِرٌ`; 73:20 `ثُلُثَهُ/يُقَدِّرُ/صَّلَوٰةَ`","synthesis":"Each motif occupies a kitchen role: supports stabilize the vessel, fire heats it, cloth protects the handler, cutting portions the ingredient, and premature baking names failed timing. The scene supplies the ordinary productive counterpart to infernal heat.","ayah_refs":["73:11","73:12","73:14","73:17","73:18","73:20"]},{"parent_channel":"P2 - Hearth and Meal Preparation","parent_semantic_invariant":"A meal is made by assembling a hearth, applying heat, cutting material, and controlling concentration.","parent_surprising_reach":"A pot, third hearthstone, lifting cloth, cut meat, fire, hastily baked dough, reduced drink, and measured sips form an ordinary kitchen whose operations are inverted by the punitive meal.","key":"B","name":"Reducing, sweetening, and serving drink","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Natural sweets are gathered, a liquid is cooked down or blended to control strength, and the result is judged and consumed in measured sips.","active_motifs":"honey retained in its comb (`quranic:root_000822:B007/m01`); sweet gum gathered from trees (`quranic:root_001096:B007/m01`); drink reduced until one third remains (`quranic:root_000203:B008/m01`); diluting strong drink with water (`quranic:root_001200:B007/m01`); tasting and consuming (`quranic:root_000934:B001/m01`); sweetness and palatability (`quranic:root_000994:B001/m01`); a sip or breath of drinking (`quranic:root_001533:B006/m01`); oily dregs or molten residue (`quranic:root_001452:B003/m01`); cooking vessel (`quranic:root_001205:B007/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"73:11 `مَهِّلْهُمْ`; 73:13 `طَعَامًا/عَذَابًا`; 73:15 `شَٰهِدًا`; 73:20 `ثُلُثَهُ/أَنفُسِكُم/يُقَدِّرُ/يُقَٰتِلُونَ/غَفُورٌ`","synthesis":"Honey and tree gum supply sweetness, reduction concentrates, dilution softens, tasting judges, and the sip portions intake. The molten-dregs sense creates a dark bridge back to punitive heat, while palatable sweetness directly reverses the choking meal.","ayah_refs":["73:11","73:13","73:15","73:20"]},{"parent_channel":"P2 - Prestige, Luxury, and Its Reversal","parent_semantic_invariant":"Social distinction is constructed through comfort, rank, reputation, display, and claims of superiority, then exposed to reversal.","parent_surprising_reach":"Crown, leaders-as-mountains, fame, padded bodily display, dispersal, abasement, and scarcity make luxury a fragile social construction rather than a stable possession.","key":"A","name":"Rank, comfort, and recognized excellence","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"A privileged person enjoys ease, occupies an advanced rank, and receives public recognition as excellent.","active_motifs":"blessing and good condition (`quranic:root_001525:B001/m01`); softness and luxurious living (`quranic:root_001525:B002/m01`); firstness and precedence in rank (`quranic:root_000067:B001/m01`); degree and distinction (`quranic:root_001163:B002/m01`); honor and social sanctity (`quranic:root_001029:B010/m01`); selected excellence (`quranic:root_000452:B002/m01`); beauty opposed to ugliness (`quranic:root_000323:B001/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"73:11 `أُو۟لِى/ٱلنَّعْمَةِ`; 73:20 `فَضْلِ/خَيْرًا/أَعْظَمَ`","synthesis":"Comfort, priority, distinction, honor, selection, and beauty form a social rank scene. The surface targets are not simply wealthy; they are possessors of an entire apparatus that makes privilege visible and credible.","ayah_refs":["73:11","73:20"]},{"parent_channel":"P2 - Prestige, Luxury, and Its Reversal","parent_semantic_invariant":"Social distinction is constructed through comfort, rank, reputation, display, and claims of superiority, then exposed to reversal.","parent_surprising_reach":"Crown, leaders-as-mountains, fame, padded bodily display, dispersal, abasement, and scarcity make luxury a fragile social construction rather than a stable possession.","key":"C","name":"Dispersal and abasement","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A privileged collective loses cohesion, status, and secure abundance under a severe event.","active_motifs":"ostrich-like dispersal and loss of collective strength (`quranic:root_001525:B008/m01`); scarcity and abasement (`quranic:root_001251:B001/m01`); others replacing or following the first (`quranic:root_000019:B001/m01`); a calamitous event (`quranic:root_001700:B003/m01`); harmful consequence (`quranic:root_001619:B002/m01`); baseness and lowered rank (`quranic:root_000493:B003/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"73:11 `ٱلنَّعْمَةِ/قَلِيلًا`; 73:14,17 `يَوْمَ/يَوْمًا`; 73:16 `وَبِيلًا`; 73:20 `ءَاخَرُونَ/أَدْنَىٰ`","synthesis":"Reversal acts socially as well as materially: the group scatters, the first gives way to others, abundance contracts, and rank falls. This process answers the apparent permanence of luxury.","ayah_refs":["73:11","73:14","73:16","73:20"]},{"parent_channel":"P2 - Governance, Levy, and Deterrent Liability","parent_semantic_invariant":"Rule maintains a household or polity through care, contracts, assessed transfers, testimony, and sanctions that establish responsibility.","parent_surprising_reach":"The Pharaoh precedent activates not only kingship but a full administrative frame of household allegiance, tribute, tax, covenant, wage, surety, and exemplary punishment.","key":"A","name":"Household rule and sustaining authority","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"A ruler or household head gathers dependents, governs their affairs, and assumes responsibility for their maintenance.","active_motifs":"household and followers returning to a head (`quranic:root_000067:B003/m01`); governance and administrative repair (`quranic:root_000067:B004/m01`); ownership and sovereignty (`quranic:root_000532:B001/m01`); nurture and completion (`quranic:root_000532:B002/m01`); care and rule (`quranic:root_001273:B004/m01`); surety for another (`quranic:root_001332:B003/m01`); possessor or holder of a quality (`quranic:root_000527:B001/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"73:11 `أُو۟لِى`; 73:14,18,20 `كَانَتِ/كَانَ/يَكُونُ`; 73:19-20 `رَبِّهِ/رَبَّكَ`; surface anchor unavailable for `quranic:root_000527:B001` because `ذ و و` is missing from root coverage","synthesis":"Rule is rooted in a returning household relation, then specified as care, repair, maintenance, and surety. The possessor construction adds the status by which authority and privilege are socially attributed.","ayah_refs":["73:11","73:14","73:19"]},{"parent_channel":"P2 - Governance, Levy, and Deterrent Liability","parent_semantic_invariant":"Rule maintains a household or polity through care, contracts, assessed transfers, testimony, and sanctions that establish responsibility.","parent_surprising_reach":"The Pharaoh precedent activates not only kingship but a full administrative frame of household allegiance, tribute, tax, covenant, wage, surety, and exemplary punishment.","key":"C","name":"Testimony establishing sanction","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"A violation is established by witness, attributed to its actor, and answered with a deterrent consequence.","active_motifs":"knowledgeable testimony (`quranic:root_000822:B002/m01`); attribution of falsehood (`quranic:root_001290:B002/m01`); accountability for an offense (`quranic:root_000018:B002/m01`); deterrent punishment (`quranic:root_001554:B003/m01`); painful sanction (`quranic:root_000994:B005/m01`); harmful consequence (`quranic:root_001619:B002/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"73:11 `ٱلْمُكَذِّبِينَ`; 73:12 `أَنكَالًا`; 73:13 `عَذَابًا`; 73:15 `شَٰهِدًا`; 73:16 `أَخَذْنَٰهُ/وَبِيلًا`","synthesis":"Witness establishes the event, attribution identifies the violation, accountability attaches it to the actor, and sanction converts judgment into deterrence. The sequence makes punishment an institutional act rather than raw force.","ayah_refs":["73:11","73:12","73:13","73:15","73:16"]},{"parent_channel":"P2 - Animal Drive in Racing and Breeding","parent_semantic_invariant":"Animal force is expressed as directed forward drive, whether toward a finish, a mate, or productive offspring.","parent_surprising_reach":"Messenger-sending, precedence, ease, deterrence, and productive increase are all pressured by a detailed equine and herd mechanics.","key":"A","name":"Gait, racing order, and the sore hoof","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A horse is urged into smooth movement, exposes its withers, advances in race order, and may shorten stride to protect a sore hoof.","active_motifs":"smooth easy gait (`quranic:root_000563:B003/m01`); successive detachments (`quranic:root_000563:B005/m01`); the horse’s muzzle and requested run (`quranic:root_000934:B009/m01`); elevated withers beneath the rider’s hand (`quranic:root_001285:B003/m01`); a prancing horse (`quranic:root_000138:B007/m01`); a camel advancing by its foreleg gait (`quranic:root_000009:B009/m01`); courageous advance (`quranic:root_001207:B006/m01`); light responsive movement (`quranic:root_001694:B005/m01`); protecting a painful hoof (`quranic:root_001677:B003/m01`); the second horse following the winner (`quranic:root_000879:B006/m01`); an animal running and then stopping (`quranic:root_001290:B007/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"73:11 `ٱلْمُكَذِّبِينَ`; 73:13 `طَعَامًا`; 73:14 `كَثِيبًا`; 73:15-16 `أَرْسَلْنَا/رَسُولًا/وَبِيلًا`; 73:17 `تَتَّقُونَ`; 73:20 `يَسَّرَ/تُقَدِّمُوا/صَّلَوٰةَ`","synthesis":"The scene specifies body part, handling point, gait, order, motivation, and injury response. Ease becomes a quality of movement, while stopping can signal either failure or protective adaptation.","ayah_refs":["73:11","73:13","73:14","73:15","73:17","73:20"]},{"parent_channel":"P2 - Cutting, Shaping, and Material Finish","parent_semantic_invariant":"Raw material becomes a finished object through cutting, striking, grinding, weaving, covering, and surface treatment.","parent_surprising_reach":"The language of action and fulfilled promise activates an artisan workshop containing shears, adze, mallet, grinding stone, textile, pile, coating, and tanning dose.","key":"B","name":"Textile covering and surface treatment","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A woven surface receives nap, coating, dye, drape, or tanning treatment that changes appearance and durability.","active_motifs":"basic covering (`quranic:root_001307:B001/m01`); raised nap or hair on cloth (`quranic:root_001096:B003/m01`); draped garment (`quranic:root_001163:B005/m01`); a small tanning dose (`quranic:root_001533:B007/m01`); dyed cloth whose appearance deceives (`quranic:root_001290:B009/m01`); well-made weaving (`quranic:root_000217:B007/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"73:11 `ٱلْمُكَذِّبِينَ`; 73:14 `ٱلْجِبَالُ`; 73:17 `كَفَرْتُمْ`; 73:20 `فَضْلِ/أَنفُسِكُم/غَفُورٌ`","synthesis":"Weaving creates the substrate, nap and tanning alter its physical surface, dye changes its visual claim, and draping puts the finish into use. The deceptive cloth provides a concrete analogy for covering that misrepresents what lies beneath.","ayah_refs":["73:11","73:14","73:17","73:20"]},{"parent_channel":"P2 - Collective Assembly, Partition, and Dispersal","parent_semantic_invariant":"A collective can gather as a whole, divide into functional portions, move in successive groups, or lose cohesion.","parent_surprising_reach":"Crowd, herd, party, gathered whole, successive detachment, leftward branch, and ostrich-like scattering provide a precise social morphology.","key":"A","name":"Gathering into a whole","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"Individuals gather into a crowd or party whose members are treated as one operational whole.","active_motifs":"a party or group of people (`quranic:root_000957:B004/m01`); gathering parts into one (`quranic:root_001210:B001/m01`); a multitude likened to a mountain (`quranic:root_000217:B002/m01`); large gathered groups (`quranic:root_000532:B004/m01`); a people or social body (`quranic:root_001273:B001/m01`); the whole crowd without exception (`quranic:root_001096:B008/m01`); a place filled tightly by people (`quranic:root_001091:B002/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"73:11 `ٱلْمُكَذِّبِينَ`; 73:13 `غُصَّةٍ`; 73:14 `ٱلْجِبَالُ`; 73:19-20 `رَبِّهِ/رَبَّكَ/طَآئِفَةٌ/تَقُومُ/غَفُورٌ/قُرْءَانِ`","synthesis":"Gathering scales from party to people to mountain-like multitude, while crowding names the spatial consequence of density. “The whole crowd” closes the scene by removing residual outsiders.","ayah_refs":["73:11","73:13","73:14","73:19"]},{"parent_channel":"P2 - Collective Assembly, Partition, and Dispersal","parent_semantic_invariant":"A collective can gather as a whole, divide into functional portions, move in successive groups, or lose cohesion.","parent_surprising_reach":"Crowd, herd, party, gathered whole, successive detachment, leftward branch, and ostrich-like scattering provide a precise social morphology.","key":"B","name":"Portioning and scattering the collective","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"A whole is divided into a party and “others,” sent in successive units, directed to a side, or dispersed in defeat.","active_motifs":"a piece or portion of a larger thing (`quranic:root_000957:B004/m02`); another group after or apart from the first (`quranic:root_000019:B001/m01`); passing a place to one side (`quranic:root_001217:B002/m01`); taking the left side (`quranic:root_001694:B004/m01`); successive groups sent one after another (`quranic:root_000563:B005/m01`); collective dispersal and loss of strength (`quranic:root_001525:B008/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"73:11 `ٱلنَّعْمَةِ`; 73:15-16 `أَرْسَلْنَا/ٱلرَّسُولَ`; 73:20 `طَآئِفَةٌ/ءَاخَرُونَ/قَرْضًا/تَيَسَّرَ`","synthesis":"Division can be organized or catastrophic. Party, side, and succession produce functional units; dispersal breaks the relation that made the collective effective.","ayah_refs":["73:11","73:15","73:20"]},{"parent_channel":"P2 - Inward Self, Desire, and Breath","parent_semantic_invariant":"The self mediates intention, attraction, emotion, bodily life, and the need for relief.","parent_surprising_reach":"“Self” extends from inward purpose to breath, blood, living soul, precious competition, grief, love, anger, and release from constriction.","key":"A","name":"Inward purpose and affect","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"The self forms an intention, is drawn toward a valued object, and experiences love, grief, anger, or rivalry.","active_motifs":"inward purpose and discernment (`quranic:root_001533:B013/m01`); attraction of the self toward a thing (`quranic:root_000831:B005/m01`); love or grief felt inwardly (`quranic:root_001626:B004/m01`); anger and resentment (`quranic:root_001626:B005/m01`); precious value provoking competition (`quranic:root_001533:B010/m01`); the self characterized as deceptive (`quranic:root_001290:B008/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"73:11 `ٱلْمُكَذِّبِينَ`; 73:16,19 `أَخَذْنَٰهُ/ٱتَّخَذَ`; 73:19 `شَآءَ`; 73:20 `أَنفُسِكُم/تَجِدُوهُ`","synthesis":"Intention supplies direction, attraction supplies motive, and affect registers the result. Competition and self-deception show how perceived value can distort that inward process.","ayah_refs":["73:11","73:16","73:19","73:20"]},{"parent_channel":"P2 - Relinquishment and Transferred Handling","parent_semantic_invariant":"Releasing an affair changes who holds, answers for, restrains, or submits it.","parent_surprising_reach":"Leaving is not a single empty gesture: it can transfer accountability to a sovereign guarantor, recoil in weakness, impose restraint, disavow an attachment, or end in submission.","key":"A","name":"Leaving an affair to accountable custody","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"One actor releases an affair while another assumes ownership, accountability, and surety for its handling.","active_motifs":"leaving a thing (`quranic:root_001638:B003/m01`); holding a person accountable for fault (`quranic:root_000018:B002/m01`); ownership and sovereignty (`quranic:root_000532:B001/m01`); surety and undertaking another’s care (`quranic:root_001332:B003/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"73:11 `وَذَرْنِى`; 73:16,19 `أَخَذْنَٰهُ/ٱتَّخَذَ`; 73:19-20 `رَبِّهِ/رَبَّكَ`; 73:14,18,20 `كَانَتِ/كَانَ/يَكُونُ`","synthesis":"Relinquishment does not erase the affair; it moves it under a different holder. Accountability specifies judgment, sovereignty specifies authority, and surety specifies continued care, turning “leave me” into a transfer of operative control.","ayah_refs":["73:11","73:16","73:19","73:14"]},{"parent_channel":"P2 - Bodily Signs of Crisis","parent_semantic_invariant":"Acute crisis becomes legible through involuntary motion and visible changes in face, eye, lip, and hair.","parent_surprising_reach":"Cosmic shaking and infernal heat are reframed as somatic diagnostics: convulsion, pulse, fear-tremor, anger-flush, jutting eye, eye-film, cleft lip, facial distortion, and greying.","key":"A","name":"Convulsion and heated anger","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"Fear or anger produces tremor, involuntary motion, pulse, and heat visible at the face.","active_motifs":"a bodily tremor or shaking (`quranic:root_000025:B008/m01`); possessed involuntary head and body movement (`quranic:root_000025:B012/m01`); disturbed pulsing or agitation (`quranic:root_000906:B012/m01`); trembling from fear or anger (`quranic:root_001251:B005/m01`); felt anger (`quranic:root_001626:B005/m01`); a face blazing with anger (`quranic:root_000225:B004/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"73:11 `قَلِيلًا`; 73:12 `جَحِيمًا`; 73:14,20 `ٱلْأَرْضُ/أَرْضِ`; 73:20 `يَضْرِبُونَ/تَجِدُوهُ`","synthesis":"Tremor supplies the gross motion, pulse the internal rhythm, and anger the affective heat that reaches the face. The earth’s convulsion and the fire’s blaze are thereby materialized as a body losing voluntary ## Your response

Write in tr. Produce your response as three parts, in this order. If you return one combined response, label the parts clearly. If an orchestrator asks you to write the parts into separate files, omit wrapper labels from the prose and evidence files; the file path already supplies the label.

1. **The prose** — per the output contract in `COMMENTARY_SPEC.md` §5 and the task document above: continuous, single voice, no provenance markers, no headers named after evidence layers.
2. **The evidence surface** — separate from the prose, addressable per phrase, per `PRINCIPLES.md` §12.
3. A section headed exactly `=== PROMPT FRICTION ===` reporting honestly where the specification above was unclear, self-contradictory, underspecified, or impossible to follow, and any point where you had to invent a rule to proceed. Report this section every time, even if the run went cleanly — say so explicitly if you found nothing. This is how the specification gets improved.

## V2 Rendering Profile — 5.6 Sol High

This run tests whether `5.6-sol-high` can increase lexical depth while preserving
reader-facing clarity.

Increase depth through semantic precision, not additional bulk.

Before drafting, silently build a coverage ledger:

- include every `must_integrate` topic;
- include every `candidate` with a reader payoff not already expressed;
- if `v12_reader_responses` is absent because the default workflow retired
  per-ayah focus runs, do not infer a `stage_00` isolated response or
  confidence movement;
- if `v12_focus_trace_hermetic` is present, use its `baseline_models`,
  `context_deltas`, and `surprising_valid_outliers` as reconstructed
  before/after evidence. Do not call it a staged reveal transcript. Preserve
  surprising outliers when they remain anchored in this ayah, especially
  secondary split-root activations;
- include a regular reader-walk, plus/minus-5 reader-walk, whole-surah reading,
  or cross-run-publication item only when it adds a distinct retrospective
  insight or coverage check;
- use branch inventories to support explanations; they create no standalone
  prose obligation. When fallback inventories are present, treat them as
  restricted to this ayah's roots and anchored citations, not as a whole-surah
  obligation.
- treat `root_lexicon` as full dictionary/gloss support, not branch-filtered.
  It uses QAC-to-Furuq root mapping; when a QAC root is split, non-dominant
  Furuq targets are included as additional root entries and recorded in
  coverage. If multiple QAC roots map to the same Furuq root, the shared entry
  records all source roots in `qac_roots_ar` / `qac_root_mappings`; use those
  fields for evidence attribution when present, not the legacy single `root_ar`
  alone. Dictionary/gloss branches are evidence support, not independent
  obligations, unless tied to this ayah's word, topic, reader payoff, or cited
  relation. Full field means all distinct reader payoffs from activated
  material, not every dictionary branch for the root.
- if `channel_generated_outputs` lists quran-data files and this run gives you
  file access, read only those listed files when channel-family/path detail is
  necessary. Treat them as candidate/family/path evidence, not as an adjudicated
  channel ledger. State B channel restrictions still apply.

For each critical word, preserve these distinct layers when available:

1. local grammatical work;
2. locally selected sense;
3. coherent pressure supplied by activated or cited root branches;
4. one form, sound, rarity, or variant observation with a unique payoff;
5. later contextual change, compressed to its final reader-visible result.

Do not flatten these layers into a general metaphor. Deepen generic summaries by
naming the exact lexical mechanism. Ordering grammar and local meaning first is
grounding, not truth-ranking.

At first mention of an ayah word, use a structured Arabic surface span:
`{ar:surface_form, tr:Turkish-readable transliteration, gloss:target-language meaning}`.
Use the same full span again when the prose returns to that word after moving to
another word or another paragraph. Inside one short local sequence, after a full
span has just been given, a Turkish label or transliteration is enough. Explain
every technical term in the same sentence. Keep raw roots, root skeletons,
letter-by-letter root transliterations, and branch IDs in the evidence surface.
In prose, attach root discussion to the surface word:
`{ar:ٱلْعَادِيَاتِ, tr:el-âdiyât, gloss:koşup atılanlar} kelimesinin bağlı
olduğu kök alanı...`, not `ʿ-d-v kökü...`.

A paragraph may begin with the ayah's surface, a concrete image, or a
reader-facing claim. It may not begin with a bare grammar label or stacked
abstractions. Prefer "Âyet önce hamdi Allah'a verir; sabit ad cümlesi bu hamdi
yerleşik bir hüküm olarak taşır" over "Bu âyet, tek bir isim cümlesiyle yerleşik
bir hüküm kurar."

For this short ayah, keep at least two-thirds of the prose on its own wording.
Compress all later developments into at most three paragraphs and end with one
plain synthesis paragraph.

Use `v12_cross_run_publication`, if present, only as a compact coverage/priority
check derived from regular and plus/minus-5 reader runs. Do not copy it as prose,
and do not let it override local bundle evidence.

Use an explicit negative predicate only to correct a likely misconception,
protect the primary sense from replacement, or preserve live counter-evidence.
Default ceiling for this ayah: three. Never stack two negatives in one sentence.
During final revision, rewrite all other negatives as positive predication.
Do not drive negation to zero by default: if no explicit negative is needed,
include a profile-specific style audit in friction confirming that there was no
live misconception, replacement risk, or counter-evidence requiring one.

Pass only if every paragraph has one distinct reader payoff and the prose remains
clear with the evidence surface closed.
